## Summary

Current literal chemical authorities and the exact deposited SID144206451 graph match the retained achiral psilocybin identity. The bounded assay review preserves 25 emitted calls from 28 raw rows and their five PMID and 20 AID references; deposited identity does not prove physical purity, species or construct equivalence. Occurrence assertions remain only partly verified, including mixed negative-collection and cited-positive evidence. Detailed production and locus claims remain scientifically unreviewed; infectious-screen descriptions and protocols are excluded from the read.

provenance: supported. Targets: CHEBI:8614.

Other positive species in the source remain distinct.

evidence: supported. Targets: CHEBI:8614.

The 1984 abstract supports some named occurrences but does not resolve every imported species triple; abstract absence is not negative proof.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Context establishes mechanical owner/history/source equality, literal identifier routing and computed classification. Primary production, exact stock and congener claims retain their source-specific gaps. Record 388 detailed production/locus science remains unreviewed; its assay metadata does not establish physical purity, direct binding or exact construct applicability.
- Biological source applicability is separate from chemical serialization, physical sample purity and independent absolute-hand validation. Actual intake equality does not broaden the source read.
- All 76 raw LOTUS triples and 25 emitted occurrences are mechanically accounted for, but not all primary occurrence assertions were scientifically verified.
- Detailed production/locus content is deliberately scientifically unreviewed; no genes, constructs, procedures or optimization are compiled.
- The separate root assay review covers 25 emitted/28 raw calls; this biological section reuses the separate assay peer and does not claim sample purity or exact construct proof.
- Assay scientific conclusions are reused from the explicit root/independent scope. No infectious-screen descriptions or protocols are read here; deposited inactive calls remain metadata only.
- Authority matches establish the representations actually returned at their retained access times; they are not independent experimental structure or absolute-hand determinations. Organism, locus, classification and history claims are assessed separately.
- Strictly chemical identity and published provenance scope only. No synthesis, culture/production, genes/constructs, extraction instructions, potency optimization or infectious-assay implementation is reviewed or compiled. Deposited graph equality is not physical sample purity or biological protocol, species, target or endpoint equivalence. Root owns the25 emitted and28 raw assay-source judgments; three raw-only rows are not promoted.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- FailedQC001 is retained for an explicit separate network-enabled retry; no artifact will be overwritten.
- Fresh QC attempt001 failed on public canonical-artifact DNS retrieval after lint and before downstream tests. Its actual exit1/log/input capture/root observation remain pinned; the selected successful canonical QC attempt002 is separately timed. No individual-review validation failure or counter reset is claimed.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- Configured consistency only; independent scientific claim support and unrestricted remote authority refresh are not implied.
- 499non-blocking identifier-label warnings,35configured exceptions and4898no-adapter skips remain unverified within this gate.
- The QC test suite reported four skipped tests and two dependency deprecation warnings; this pass does not assert skipped checks ran.
- Canonical reproduction retains five unresolved assay-organism annotations,42 withheld nonsingle-organism producer claims and161 configured taxon-ID rewrites.
- This QC is a configured consistency gate, not an independent primary-source biological or stereochemical verification.
- Native baseline gates establish configured consistency, not independent biological production, exact stereochemistry or primary-source adequacy.
- The full configured gate logs retain warnings, skipped tests, identifier exceptions and no-adapter pairs; a successful command does not convert these into verified scientific claims.
- Ontology caches may be reused by the canonical identifier-label gate. Fresh command execution is not proof every remote authority was refreshed.
- Every tracked byte stream/mode and ignored-inclusive corpus/review YAML population was checked; external caches and generated bytecode are not asserted to be independently byte-pinned.
- No scientific owner, inventory, curation table, native status or history was edited. Actions are proposed work.
- Baseline gates retain their actual original receipts after byte-for-byte input verification; target strict validation ran afresh. Scientific failed or unavailable checks are distinct from bundle validation.
- Scientific conclusions integrate the chemical, biological and provenance assessments; source access and unresolved scopes remain explicit.

  reuses the separate assay peer and does not claim sample purity or exact construct
  proof.

- Authority matches establish the representations actually returned at their retained

  Organism, locus, classification and history claims are assessed separately.
- Strictly chemical identity and published provenance scope only. No synthesis, culture/production,

  implementation is reviewed or compiled. Deposited graph equality is not physical
  sample purity or biological protocol, species, target or endpoint equivalence. Root
